  (function () {
    if (customElements.get('lit-ssr-dedupe-style')){
      return;
    };

    const supportsAdoptingStyleSheets =
      window.ShadowRoot &&
      (window.ShadyCSS === undefined || window.ShadyCSS.nativeShadow) &&
      'adoptedStyleSheets' in Document.prototype &&
      'replaceSync' in CSSStyleSheet.prototype;

    class StyleModule extends HTMLElement {
      static styleCache = new Map();
      static pendingShadowRoots = new Map();

      _cachedStyleElement = undefined;
      _cachedSS = undefined;
      _hasValidStyles = false;

      get constructableStyleSheet() {
        if (this._cachedSS && this._hasValidStyles) {
          return this._cachedSS;
        }

        if (!this._cachedSS) {
          this._cachedSS = new CSSStyleSheet();
        }

        if (this._cachedStyleElement) {
          this._cachedSS.replaceSync(this._cachedStyleElement.textContent ?? '');
          this._cachedStyleElement.remove();
          this._cachedStyleElement = undefined;
          this._hasValidStyles = true;
        }

        return this._cachedSS;
      }

      connectedCallback() {
        const styleId = this.getAttribute('style-id');
        const styleEl = this.previousElementSibling;
        const isStyleEl = styleEl && styleEl.tagName === 'STYLE';
        let cachedEntry = StyleModule.styleCache.get(styleId);
        const isDefinition = cachedEntry == null;
        const shadowRoot = this.parentNode;

        if (isDefinition) {
          StyleModule.styleCache.set(styleId, (cachedEntry = this));
          if (isStyleEl) {
            this._cachedStyleElement = styleEl;
          }
        } else if (isStyleEl && !cachedEntry._hasValidStyles) {
          cachedEntry._cachedStyleElement = styleEl;
        }

        if (!supportsAdoptingStyleSheets) {
          const defStyle = cachedEntry._cachedStyleElement;

          if (!isDefinition && defStyle) {
            this.before(defStyle.cloneNode(true));
          }
          return;
        }

        const sheet = cachedEntry.constructableStyleSheet;

        if (!isDefinition && isStyleEl) {
          styleEl.remove();
        }

        if (!cachedEntry._hasValidStyles) {
          const pending = StyleModule.pendingShadowRoots.get(styleId) || [];

          pending.push(shadowRoot);
          StyleModule.pendingShadowRoots.set(styleId, pending);
          return;
        }

        shadowRoot.adoptedStyleSheets.push(sheet);

        const pending = StyleModule.pendingShadowRoots.get(styleId);
        if (pending) {
          for (const pendingShadowRoot of pending) {
            pendingShadowRoot.adoptedStyleSheets.push(sheet);
          }
          StyleModule.pendingShadowRoots.delete(styleId);
        }

        this.remove();
      }
    }

    customElements.define('lit-ssr-dedupe-style', StyleModule);
  })();

    Object.hasOwnProperty.call(HTMLTemplateElement.prototype, "shadowRoot");

  if (!hasNativeDsd) {
      document.body.setAttribute('dsd-pending', 'true');
  }

“Heel Holland Hacked”: waarom oefenen meer oplevert dan beleid (met Kelvin Rorive)","Wat gebeurt er als je organisatie ineens midden in een cybercrisis zit en niemand echt weet wat te doen?
